```text
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";

describe("WorkerEntryContentUrlManager", () => {
  it("loads one object URL per worker entry and revokes it after the final release", async () => {
    const module = await import("@/interface/home/WorkerEntryContentUrlManager").catch(() => null);
    expect(module).not.toBeNull();
    if (!module) return;

    const createObjectUrl = vi.fn(() => "blob:generated-image");
    const revokeObjectUrl = vi.fn();
    const load = vi.fn(async () => new Blob([new Uint8Array([1, 2, 3])], { type: "image/png" }));
    const manager = new module.WorkerEntryContentUrlManager({ createObjectUrl, revokeObjectUrl });
    const reference = { workerId: "worker-1", entryId: "image-1" };

    manager.acquire(reference, load);
    manager.acquire(reference, load);
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(manager.getContentState(reference).status).toBe("loaded"));

    expect(load).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
    expect(manager.getContentState(reference)).toMatchObject({
      status: "loaded",
      url: "blob:generated-image",
      error: null,
    });

    manager.release(reference);
    expect(revokeObjectUrl).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    manager.release(reference);
    expect(revokeObjectUrl).toHaveBeenCalledWith("blob:generated-image");
    expect(manager.getContentState(reference).status).toBe("idle");
  });

  it("rejects a successful non-image response instead of creating a broken image URL", async () => {
    const module = await import("@/interface/home/WorkerEntryContentUrlManager").catch(() => null);
    expect(module).not.toBeNull();
    if (!module) return;

    const createObjectUrl = vi.fn(() => "blob:not-an-image");
    const manager = new module.WorkerEntryContentUrlManager({
      createObjectUrl,
      revokeObjectUrl: vi.fn(),
    });
    const reference = { workerId: "worker-1", entryId: "image-1" };

    manager.acquire(reference, async () => new Blob([JSON.stringify({ entries: [] })], {
      type: "application/json",
    }));
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(manager.getContentState(reference).status).toBe("error"));

    expect(createObjectUrl).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(manager.getContentState(reference)).toMatchObject({
      status: "error",
      url: "",
    });
  });
});
```

```json
  "content": "import { describe, expect, it } from \"vitest\";\nimport {\n  IMAGE_CONTENT_DATA_CHARS,\n  elideInlineImageContentData,\n  inlineImageContentData,\n  preserveInlineImageContentData,\n} from \"@/shared/worker-entries\";\nimport { buildGeneratedImagesActivity } from \"@/components/terminal/generated-image-activity\";\nimport type { WorkerEntry } from \"@/shared/worker-entries\";\n\nconst TRUNCATED = \"iVBORw0K\\n[truncated 141592 chars]\";\n\nfunction imageRaw(data: string) {\n  return { content: { type: \"image\", data, mimeType: \"image/png\" } };\n}\n\ndescribe(\"inlineImageContentData\", () => {\n  it(\"finds the payload on content entries only\", () => {\n    expect(inlineImageContentData(\"agent_content\", imageRaw(\"abc\"))).toBe(\"abc\");\n    expect(inlineImageContentData(\"user_content\", imageRaw(\"abc\"))).toBe(\"abc\");\n    expect(inlineImageContentData(\"tool_call\", imageRaw(\"abc\"))).toBeNull();\n    expect(inlineImageContentData(\"agent_content\", { content: { type: \"text\", text: \"hi\" } })).toBeNull();\n    expect(inlineImageContentData(\"agent_content\", undefined)).toBeNull();\n    // Tool results nest content as an array; that is not an inline image.\n    expect(inlineImageContentData(\"agent_content\", { content: [imageRaw(\"abc\")] })).toBeNull();\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe(\"preserveInlineImageContentData\", () => {\n  it(\"restores the payload the generic compactor truncated\", () => {\n    const original = imageRaw(\"A\".repeat(10_000));\n    const compacted = imageRaw(TRUNCATED);\n\n    const result = preserveInlineImageContentData(\"agent_content\", original, compacted) as {\n      content: { data: string; mimeType: string };\n    };\n\n    expect(result.content.data).toBe(\"A\".repeat(10_000));\n    // Everything the compactor did to the rest of the notification stands.\n    expect(result.content.mimeType).toBe(\"image/png\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"leaves non-image and oversized payloads to the compactor\", () => {\n    const compacted = imageRaw(TRUNCATED);\n    expect(preserveInlineImageContentData(\"tool_call\", imageRaw(\"A\".repeat(10_000)), compacted))\n      .toBe(compacted);\n    expect(preserveInlineImageContentData(\n      \"agent_content\",\n      imageRaw(\"A\".repeat(IMAGE_CONTENT_DATA_CHARS + 1)),\n      compacted,\n    )).toBe(compacted);\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe(\"elideInlineImageContentData\", () => {\n  it(\"swaps the payload for a pointer without disturbing other fields\", () => {\n    const entry = {\n      id: \"image-1\",\n      type: \"agent_content\",\n      raw: { sessionUpdate: \"agent_message_chunk\", ...imageRaw(\"A\".repeat(10_000)) },\n    };\n\n    const result = elideInlineImageContentData(entry, \"worker-1\") as typeof entry & {\n      raw: { sessionUpdate: string; content: Record<string, unknown> };\n    };\n\n    expect(result.raw.content.data).toBeUndefined();\n    expect(result.raw.content.mimeType).toBe(\"image/png\");\n    expect(result.raw.content.omniWorkerContent).toEqual({ workerId: \"worker-1\", entryId: \"image-1\" });\n    expect(result.raw.sessionUpdate).toBe(\"agent_message_chunk\");\n    expect(entry.raw.content.data).toBe(\"A\".repeat(10_000));\n  });\n\n  it(\"passes through entries that carry no inline image\", () => {\n    const entry = { id: \"t1\", type: \"tool_call\", raw: { content: { type: \"text\" } } };\n    expect(elideInlineImageContentData(entry, \"worker-1\")).toBe(entry);\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe(\"buildGeneratedImagesActivity\", () => {\n  it(\"resolves images through the server's content pointer\", () => {\n    const entries = [\n      {\n        id: \"image-1\",\n        seq: 1,\n        type: \"agent_content\",\n        text: \"image\",\n        timestamp: \"2026-08-24T15:12:11.870Z\",\n        raw: {\n          content: {\n            type: \"image\",\n            mimeType: \"image/png\",\n            omniWorkerContent: { workerId: \"worker-7\", entryId: \"image-1\" },\n          },\n        },\n      },\n    ] as unknown as WorkerEntry[];\n\n    const [gallery] = buildGeneratedImagesActivity(entries, \"fallback-worker\");\n\n    expect(gallery.images).toHaveLength(1);\n    expect(gallery.images[0].data).toBeUndefined();\n    expect(gallery.images[0].reference).toEqual({ workerId: \"worker-7\", entryId: \"image-1\" });\n  });\n});\n"
```

## Claude · 2026-08-24T20:12:21.814Z

Fixed all three layers. The root cause turned out to be worse than my first read: image payloads were being shredded at **two** independent truncation points, not one.

## Server — stop destroying the payload

`src/server/agent-runtime/output-store.ts` (`toLiveEntry`, `createArchiveEntry`) and `src/server/workers/output-store.ts` (`compactEntryForHistory`) both ran base64 image data through generic raw-string truncators at 4,000 chars. Both now exempt it, using the same reasoning `toLiveEntry` already applied to assistant message text — this is conversation content, not disposable runtime diagnostics.

The shared predicate, the bound, and the elision helper live in `src/shared/worker-entries.ts` so the write path, the read path, and the wire format can't drift apart. Payloads stay bounded at 25 MB decoded, matching the ceiling the reader already enforced.

## Server — keep the wire small

Storing whole images would have pushed megabytes of base64 into every transcript page. `src/runtime/http/routes/worker-entries.ts` now swaps the inline payload for a `{workerId, entryId}` pointer on all three read paths. The client branch that consumes that pointer already existed at `Terminal.tsx:1874` — it was dead code, because nothing ever produced one.

## Server — recover the existing sessions

`readWorkerEntryContent` falls back to the runtime archive when the canonical stream holds a truncated prefix. Verified against the real data: all 11 images in `84eb9cec4345` decode as valid PNGs (13 KB–117 KB).

## Client — stop swallowing the message

`src/runtime-api/request.ts` read failed `responseType: "blob"` replies as Blobs, discarding the server's JSON error. It now reads error bodies as JSON regardless of the success-path type. A new `runtimeErrorMessage` helper handles the second half: `RuntimeApiError` is a plain object literal, never an `Error`, so `String(error)` rendered every failure as `[object Object]`.

## Verification

- Typecheck clean across all four tsconfigs; eslint clean; `pnpm build:interface:web` passes.
- 15 new tests across 4 files — payload survival end-to-end, pointer elision, archive fallback, the 404 message, blob error parsing, and the manager's error text.
- Full suite: 2925 passed. Five failures are pre-existing — I stashed my changes and confirmed the identical five on a clean tree (route-fixture drift on `/api/accounts/:id/auth-operation`, a terminal CSS assertion, goal-schema, supervisor busy-timeout, conversations-sync). Two conversations-route tests failed only in the full run and pass in isolation both with and without my changes; they look order-dependent, and I did not investigate further.

Two things I deliberately did not do: the server on port 3050 is still running the old code, so restarting it is your call; and I didn't write a `docs/superpowers/learnings/` entry, though this bug fits the pattern well if you want one.
